Saenchaimuaythaigym|Tawanchai P.K.Saenchai]] was expected to the co-main event. Iraj Azizpour ONE Heavyweight Kickboxing World Grand Prix Final Added To ONE 163|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Jay Furness|date=2022-10-26|access-date=2022-10-27}} The pairing first met at [[2018 in Kunlun Fight#Kunlun Fight 69|Kunlun Fight 69]] on February 4, 2018 for the 2018 Kunlun Fight +100 kg Tournament, where Azizpour won by extra round majority decision.{{cite web |title=Kunlun Fight 69 Results: Grigorian Stuns Superbon, Azizpour Upsets Kryklia, Buakaw Dominates |url=https://combatpress.com/2018/02/kunlun-fight-69-live-stream-and-results/ |publisher=Combat Press |date=2018-02-03|access-date=2022-11-06}} Their second meeting took place at [[2019 in Kunlun Fight#Kunlun Fight 80|Kunlun Fight 80]] on February 24, 2019 for the 2019 Kunlun Fight +100 kg Tournament, where Kryklia won by unanimous decision.{{cite web |title=Kunlun Fight 80 Results: Kryklia Claims 100+kg Tournament, Kiria Edges Toutouh to Win 2018 70-kilogram Tournament |url=https://combatpress.com/2019/02/kunlun-fight-80-grand-finals-live-stream-and-results/ |publisher=Combat Press |date=2019-02-26|access-date=2022-11-06}} The pairing was previously scheduled to headlined at [[2021 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: NextGen|ONE: NextGen]] for the inaugural [[ONE Heavyweight World Championship|ONE Heavyweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout, but Kryklia withdraw due to an undisclosed medical issue.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-one-nextgen-azizpour-replaces-aygen-main-event|title=Roman Kryklia vs. Iraj Azizpour trilogy Wakamatsu weighed in at 139 lbs, 4 pounds over the limit, Shinagawa came in at 126 lbs, 1 pounds over the limit, and Hirata failed weight and hydration. Botelho.{{Cite web|url=https://beyondkick.com/news/one-163-weigh-in-results-three-fighters-fail-weight-and-hydration-akimoto-and-petchtanong-both-pass|title=ONE 163 Weigh-in Results: Three Fighters was broadcast live on [[Amazon Prime Video]]. [[Colorado]] was chosen for the US debut because its athletic commission was the first to approve ONE's rule set.{{cite web|author=Adam Stern|url=https://www.sportsbusinessjournal.com/Daily/Issues/2022/12/05/Leagues-and-Governing-Bodies/one-championship.aspx|title=ONE Championship to host its first U.S. event|website=[[Sports Business Journal]]|date=2022-12-05|access-date=2023-01-06}}\n\nA [[ONE Flyweight World Championship]] trilogy bout between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] (also former [[List of UFC champions#Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] and [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Flyweight Grand Prix bracket|2019 ONE Flyweight World Grand Prix Champion]]) and [[Adriano Moraes (fighter)|Adriano Moraes]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/12/5/23494598/demetrious-johnson-vs-adriano-moraes-3-to-headline-one-championship-u-s-debut-on-may-5|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Adriano Moraes 3 to headline ONE Championship U.S. debut on May 5|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=December 5, 2022|access-date=2022-12-05}} The pairing first met at [[2021 in ONE Championship#ONE on TNT 1|ONE on TNT 1]] on April 7, 2021, where Moraes won by knockout in the second round.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31212935/demetrious-johnson-knocked-adriano-moraes-one-championship-title-bout|title=Demetrious Johnson knocked out by Adriano Moraes in One Championship title bout|work=ESPN.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=April 7, 2021|access-date=2022-12-05}} Their second meeting took place at [[ONE on Prime Video 1]] on August 27, 2022, where Johnson won by knockout in the fourth round.{{Cite web |last=Dewar |first=Val |date=August 27, 2022 |title=ONE on Prime Video: Mighty Knee Sees Demetrious Johnson Finish Adriano Moraes |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/08/27/johnson-vs-moraes-one-prime-video/ |access-date=2022-12-05 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\nA [[ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Rodtang Jitmuangnon]] and Edgar Tabares took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/02/08/rodtang-joins-one-championships-u-s-debut-faces-edgar-tabares/|title=Rodtang joins ONE Championship's U.S. debut, faces Edgar Tabares|website=Cageside [[ONE Middleweight World Championship|ONE Middleweight]] and [[ONE Light Heavyweight World Championship|Light Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Aung La Nsang]] and [[Fan Rong]] took place at the [[COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/aung-la-nsang-gets-new-opponent-at-one-on-prime-video-6/|title=Aung La Nsang gets new opponent at ONE on Prime Video 6|website=Asian MMA|date=January Background ==\nA [[List of ONE Championship champions#Featherweight World Championship|ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Chingiz Allazov]] and former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Lightweight Championship|Glory Lightweight]] champion [[Marat Grigorian]] headlined the event.{{Cite |access-date=2023-05-18}} The pair met for the first time at [[Glory 7: Milan]] in 2013, but the fight ended in a no-contest due to an illegal elbow. Their second meeting took place in [[Levallois-Perret]], [[France]] in 2014, where Grigorian won by unanimous decision.{{Cite web|url=https://boxemag.com/one-championship/chingiz-allazov-vs-marat-grigorian-3-prevu-au-one-fight-night-13/|title=Chingiz Allazov vs Marat Grigorian 3 scheduled for ONE Fight Night 13|website=Boxemag|date=18 May 2023 |access-date=2023-05-18}}\n\nA [[ONE Flyweight Submission Grappling World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Mikey Musumeci]] and current [[List of ONE Championship champions#Strawweight World Championship|ONE Strawweight Champion]] [[Jarred Brooks]] served as the event's co-headliner.{{Cite web |date=2023-07-15 |title=Mikey Musumeci to defend Flyweight Submission Grappling World title against Jarred Brooks at ONE Fight Night 13 |url=https://www.onefc.com/news/mikey-musumeci-to-defend-flyweight-submission-grappling-world-title-against-jarred-brooks-at-one-fight-night-13/ |access-date=2023-07-15 |website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home Of Martial Arts |language=en-US}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between the multiple-time [[World IBJJF Jiu-Jitsu Championship#List of winners by total titles|World IBJJF Jiu-Jitsu champion]] and [[ADCC Submission Fighting World Championship#ADCC world championships|ADCC submission fighting]] World champion [[Marcus Almeida]] and Oumar Kane took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/oumar-kane-faces-marcus-almeida-at-one-fight-night-13/|title=Oumar Kane faces Marcus Almeida at ONE Fight Night 13|website=asianmma.com|date=23 May 2023 |access-date=2023-05-23}}{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/05/22/reug-reug-vs-buchecha-announced-one-fight-night-13/|title=Reug Reug vs. Buchecha Announced for ONE Fight Night 13|website=Cageside Press|date=22 May 2023 |access-date=2023-05-22}}\n\nA featherweight kickboxing bout between the current [[List of ONE Championship champions#Featherweight World Championship|ONE Featherweight Muay Thai Champion]] [[Tawanchai P.K. Press|access-date=2023-09-15}}\n\n== Background ==\n\nA [[ONE Bantamweight Kickboxing World Championship]] for the vacant title between current [[ONE Bantamweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Bantamweight Muay Thai]] champion [[Jonathan Haggerty]] (also former [[ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Flyweight Muay Thai]] champion) and current [[ONE Bantamweight World Champion|ONE Bantamweight]] champion [[Fabr\u00edcio Andrade]] headlined the event. If successful, Haggerty and Andrade would have become the fourth fighter in ONE history to win a title in different sports (after [[Stamp Fairtex]] at [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: Call to Greatness|ONE: Call to Greatness]], [[Sam-A Gaiyanghadao]] at [[2020 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: King of the Jungle|ONE: King of the Jungle]] and [[Regian Eersel]] at [[ONE on Prime Video 3]]). |website=South China Morning Post |language=en}} Former champion [[Petchtanong Petchfergus]] was stripped of the title due to testing positive for both metenolone and boldenone, banned substance according to the [[World Anti-Doping Agency]] (WADA).{{Cite web |date=2023-07-26 |title=Petchtanong popped for PEDs, stripped of title Super Bantamweight Championship|Glory Women's Super Bantamweight Champions]] [[Anissa Meksen]] was planned for the event. However, Meksen missed the weight and hydration testing window. As a result, she will not be allowed to compete. In a social media post, she addressed her no-showing, explaining that she was on weight and wasn't ill, but cited \"family issues\" as the reason she won't be able to compete.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/anissa-meksen-no-shows-one-fight-night-6-weigh-ins/|title=Anissa Meksen no shows ONE Fight Night 6 weigh ins!|website=Asian MMA|access-date=2023-01-12}} Meksen was replaced by Anna Jarronsak to face Stamp in a women's strawweight kickboxing bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/stamp-fairtex-to-face-anna-supergirl-jaroonsak-at-one-fight-night-6/|title=Stamp Fairtex to face Anna 'Supergirl' Jaroonsak at ONE Fight Night 6|website=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Bear Farzer|date=2023-01-13|access-date=2023-01-13}}\n\nA [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and former [[List of K-1 champions#K-1 Super Welterweight Championship|K-1 Super Welterweight Champion]] (also [[2022 in ONE Championship#ONE Kickboxing Featherweight Grand Prix bracket|2022 ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Grand Prix Champion]]) [[Chingiz Allazov]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/superbon-vs-allazov-featherweight-kickboxing-world-title-fight-rebooked-for-one-on-prime-video-5/|title=Superbon Vs. Furness|date=2022-10-28|access-date=2022-10-28}} The pairing was originally booked for the co-main event at [[ONE on Prime Video 2]] and [[ONE on Prime Video 5]], but Allazov was forced to withdraw and Canada.{{Cite news |last=Bhasin |first=Kim |date=27 April 2022 |title=Amazon Signs Deal to Bring One Championship MMA Fights to Prime |work=Bloomberg |url=https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2022-04-27/amazon-amzn-brings-one-championship-mma-fights-to-prime-video |access-date=20 November 2022}}\n\n==Background==\nThe debut event of the series featured the [[List of ONE Championship champions#Flyweight World Championship|ONE Flyweight World Championship]] rematch between current three-time champion [[Adriano Moraes (fighter)|Adriano Moraes]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship|UFC Flyweight Champion]] (also [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Flyweight Grand Prix bracket|2019 ONE Flyweight World Grand Prix Champion]]) [[Demetrious Johnson]].{{cite web |last1=Hannoun |first1=Farah |title=Adriano Moraes vs. Demetrious Johnson 2 set to headline ONE Championship 161 in August |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/adriano-moraes-vs-demetrious-johnson-rematch-one-161-main-event-booked |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |date=3 June 2022 |access-date=3 June 2022}} The pair previously fought at [[2021 in ONE Championship#ONE on TNT 1|ONE on TNT 1]] in April 2021 where Moraes defeated Johnson by KO in Round 2.{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31212935/demetrious-johnson-knocked-adriano-moraes-one-championship-title-bout|title=Demetrious Johnson knocked out by Adriano Moraes in One Championship event|publisher=Sportskeeda|author=Timothy Wheaton|date=20 June 2021|access-date=23 June 2022}} A [[List of ONE Championship champions#Bantamweight World Championship|ONE Bantamweight Muay Thai World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Nong-O Gaiyanghadao]] and [[Liam Harrison (kickboxer)|Liam Harrison]] was the co-main event at the show.{{cite web |last1=Taylor |first1=Tom |title=ONE Championship 161 line-up revealed; Nong-O vs Liam Harrison added to Prime Video card |url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3184663/one-championship-161-line-revealed-nong-o-vs |website=scmp.com |date=8 July 2022 |access-date=9 July 2022}}\n\nThe event was to feature a semi-final of the [[2022 in ONE Championship#ONE Muay Thai Flyweight Grand Prix bracket|ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Grand Prix]] between current [[List of ONE Championship champions#Heavyweight World Championship|ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Champion]] [[Rodtang Jitmuangnon]] and [[Savvas Michael]] with the winner advancing to the World Grand Prix final.{{cite web |title=Rodtang vs. Michael announced for ONE 161 |url=https://www.thejakartapost.com/sports/2022/07/05/rodtang-vs-michael-announced-for-one-161.html |website=thejakartapost.com |access-date=9 July 2022}} However, Rodtang was forced to withdraw due to failed to provide a sample for the organization\u2019s mandatory hydration test and was not permitted to weigh in and was replaced by [[Panpayak Jitmuangnon]] who scheduled against Sherzod Kabutov in preliminary card.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/rodtang-withdraws-from-grand-prix-panpayak-steps-into-semifinals/|title=Rodtang at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-jonathan-haggerty-faces-amir-naseri-one-161|title=Fully recovered Jonathan Haggerty to face Amir Naseri at ONE 161 in tournament alternate|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Atilano Diaz|date=2022-07-08|access-date=2022-08-24}} However, Haggerty forced to withdraw from the contest due to a non-COVID related illness and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/jonathan-haggerty-out-updated-cards-for-one-160-and-one-on-prime-video-1/|title=Jonathan Haggerty Out; Updated Cards For ONE 160 And ONE On bouts. [[Itsuki Hirata]] weighed in at 119.25 pounds, 4.25 lbs over the [[Atomweight]] non-title fight limit. Zebaztian Kadestam weighed in at 188.5 pounds, 3.5 lbs over the [[Welterweight]] non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight. Kadestam was fined 30% of their purse, which went to their opponent Iuri Lapicus; Hirata was fined 50% of their purse, which went to their opponent Lin Heqin.{{Cite vs. Lee 3 ''''' (also known as '''''ONE Fight Night 2''''' outside North America) was a [[combat sport]] event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on October 1, 2022 at the [[Singapore Indoor Stadium]] in [[Kallang]], Singapore.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/angela-lee-vs-xiong-jing-nan-iii-to-headline-one-on-prime-video-2-on-september-30/|title=Angela Lee Vs. Xiong Jing Nan III To Headline ONE On Prime Video 2 On September 30|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Bear Frazer|date=2022-08-15|access-date=2022-08-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/8/15/23306829/angela-lee-vs-xiong-jingnan-3-for-strawweight-title-set-to-headline-one-on-prime-video-2-card|title=Angela Lee vs. Xiong Jingnan 3 for strawweight title set to headline ONE on Prime Video 2 card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-08-15|access-date=2022-08-15}}{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2022/08/16/angela-lee-vs-xiong-jing-nan-3-strawweight-title-fight-booked-one-prime-video-2/|title=Angela Lee vs. Xiong Jing Nan 3 Strawweight Title Fight Booked for ONE on Prime Video 2|publisher=Cageside Press|author=Jay Anderson|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-08-20}}{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/lee-vs-jingnan-estelarizara-el-one-on-prime-video-2/|title=Trilog\u00eda entre Angela Lee y Xiong Jingnan estelarizar\u00e1 el ONE on Prime Video 2 - MMA.uno, #1 En noticias de Artes Marciales Mixtas (MMA) en Espa\u00f1ol. |publisher=mma.uno|author=Julio Fernando Navas|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-07-20}} {{in lang|es}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[List of ONE Championship champions#Women's Strawweight World Championship|ONE Women's Strawweight World Championship]] bout and trilogy between current champion [[Xiong Jing Nan]] and current [[List of ONE Championship champions#Women's Atomweight World Championship|ONE Women's Atomweight Champion]] [[Angela Lee]] headlined the event. The pairing first met at [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: A New Era|ONE: A New Era]] on March 31, 2019 where Xiong won by knockout in the fifth round.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/xiong-jing-nan-knocks-out-angela-lee-in-historic-world-title-bout/|title=Xiong Jing Nan Knocks Out Angela Lee In Historic World Title Bout|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|date=2019-03-31|access-date=2022-08-15}} Their second meeting took place at [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Championship: Century|ONE: Century]] on October 13, 2019 in [[List of ONE Championship champions#Women's Strawweight World Championship|ONE Women's Atomweight World Championship]] bout, where Lee defended the title by submission in the fifth round.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/angela-lee-submits-xiong-jing-nan-in-the-final-seconds-to-retain-the-gold/|title=Angela Lee Submits Xiong Jing Nan In The Final Seconds To Retain The Gold|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|date=2019-10-13|access-date=2022-08-15}}\n\nA [[List of ONE Championship champions#Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship|ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and former [[List of K-1 champions#K-1 Super Welterweight Championship|K-1 Super Welterweight Champion]] (also [[2022 in ONE Championship#ONE Kickboxing Featherweight Grand Prix bracket|2022 ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Grand Prix Champion]]) [[Chingiz Allazov]] was expected to take place as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/8/16/23307690/superbon-set-to-defend-title-against-chingiz-allazov-at-one-on-prime-video-2-kickboxing-mma|title=Superbon set to defend title against Chingiz Allazov at ONE on Prime Video 2|publisher=MMA Mania|author=Drake Riggs|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-08-18}} However, Allazov forced to withdraw due to an injury and was replaced by #5 ranked contender [[Tayfun \u00d6zcan]] who scheduled against former [[Glory (kickboxing)#Glory Lightweight Championship|Glory Lightweight Champion]] [[Marat Grigorian]] in lead card and while Grigorian was booked to face Jamal Yusupov.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/chingiz-allazov-out-of-world-title-fight-with-injury-tayfun-ozcan-to-challenge-superbon/|title=Chingiz Allazov Withdraws From World Title Fight; Tayfun Ozcan To Challenge Superbon|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=John Wolcott|date=2022-09-21|access-date=2022-09-21}} In turn, Superbon and Yusupov withdrew from the event due to illness, so \u00d6zcan and Grigorian stepped back into the original pairing.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/superbon-out-marat-grigorian-faces-tayfun-ozcan/|title=Superbon out, Marat Grigorian faces Tayfun Ozcan|publisher=AsianMMA.com|date=2022-09-30|access-date=2022-09-30}}\n\nThe inaugural [[List of ONE Championship champions|ONE Flyweight Submission Grappling World Championship]] bout between [[Mikey Musumeci]] and Cleber Sousa took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-one-prime-video-2-crown-inaugural-one-flyweight-submission-grappling-world-champion?key1=googlenews\n|title=ONE on Prime Video 2 to crown the inaugural ONE flyweight submission grappling world champion featuring Mikey Musumeci and Cleber Sousa|publisher=Sportskeeda|author=Ben Imperial|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-08-18}} It was also the first submission grappling title in the promotion's history.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/one-championship-estrenara-campeonato-mundial-de-submission-grappling/|title=ONE Championship estrenar\u00e1 campeonato mundial de submission grappling - MMA.uno, #1 En noticias de Artes Marciales Mixtas (MMA) en Espa\u00f1ol. |publisher=mma.uno|author=Andr\u00e9s Lichtveld|date=2022-08-17|access-date=2022-08-20}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, two fighters missed weight for their respective bouts. [[Dangkongfah Kiatpetnoi Gym|Dangkongfah Banchamek]] weighed in at 115.25 pounds, 0.25 lbs over the atomweight non-title fight limit. Jihin Radzuan weighed in at 120.25 pounds, 5.25 lbs over the atomweight non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight. Dangkongfah was fined 20% of their purse, which went to their opponent [[Anissa Meksen]]; Radzuan was fined 30% of their purse, which went to their opponent [[Stamp Fairtex]]; [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and Jamal Yusupov have both not been medically cleared and both withdrew from the event.{{Cite web |title=ONE Championship: Angela Lee, Xiong Jingnan get physical at weigh-ins; Superbon out of title fight |url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3194414/one-championship-angela-lee-xiong-jingnan-get |date=2022-09-30 |access-date=2022-09-30 |website=South China Morning Post |author=Nicolas Atkin |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Amazon Prime Video)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight \n|[[Xiong Jing Nan]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Angela Lee]] \n|Decision (unanimous)\n|5\n|5:00\n|For the [[List of ONE Championship champions#Women's Strawweight World Championship|ONE Women's Strawweight World Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight Submission Grappling \n|[[Mikey Musumeci]]\n|def.\n|Cleber Sousa\n|Decision (unanimous)\n|1\n|10:00\n|For the inaugural [[List of ONE Championship champions#Flyweight World Championship|ONE Flyweight Submission Grappling World Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (120.75 lbs)\n|[[Stamp Fairtex]]\n|def.\n|Jihin Radzuan\n|Decision (unanimous)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Radzuan missed weight (120.25 lbs). Malaysia.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/de-ridder-vs-abdulaev-lineker-vs-andrade-mma-world-title-fights-top-one-on-prime-video-3/|title=De Ridder vs. Abdulaev, Lineker vs. Andrade MMA World web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/6/23339707/reinier-de-ridder-vs-shamil-abdulaev-john-lineker-vs-fabricio-andrade-headline-one-on-prime-video-3|title=Reinier de Ridder vs. Shamil Abdulaev, John Lineker vs. Fabricio Andrade headline ONE on Prime Video 3|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-09-06|access-date=2022-09-06}}{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/de-ridder-vs-abdulaev-sera-la-estelar-de-one-on-prime-video-3/|title=Reinier de Ridder vs. Shamil Abdulaev ser\u00e1 la estelar del ONE on Prime Video 3 - MMA.uno, #1 En noticias de Artes Marciales Mixtas (MMA) en Espa\u00f1ol. |publisher=mma.uno|author=Julio Fernando Navas|date=2022-09-06|access-date=2022-09-07 }} {{in lang|es}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally expected to be headlined by a [[List of ONE Championship champions#Middleweight World Championship|ONE Middleweight World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Reinier de Ridder]] (also the current [[List of ONE Championship champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|ONE Light Heavyweight World Champion]]) and promotional newcomer Shamil Abdulaev. However, Abdulaev forced to withdraw due to not medically cleared and the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3193733/one-championship-reinier-de-ridders-title|title=ONE Championship: Reinier de Ridder's title fight off Prime Video 3 card in Malaysia, source says|publisher=South China Morning Post|author=Nicolas Atkin|date=2022-09-25|access-date=2022-09-30}}\n\nA [[List of ONE Championship champions#Bantamweight World Championship|ONE Bantamweight World Championship]] bout between then champion [[John Lineker]] and [[Fabr\u00edcio Andrade]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/john-lineker-faces-fabricio-andrade-at-one-on-prime-video-3/|title=John Lineker faces Fabricio Andrade at ONE on Prime Video 3|publisher=AsianMMA|date=2022-09-07|access-date=2022-09-07}} At the weigh-ins, Lineker weighed in at 145.75 pounds, 0.75 pounds over the limit. As a result, Lineker was stripped of the title and only Andrade was eligible to win it.{{Cite web |date=2022-10-20 |title=John Lineker misses weight for ONE on Prime Video 3 |url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3196683/one-championship-prime-video-3-weigh-ins-and-hydration-test-results-john-lineker-misses-mark-title |access-date=2022-10-20 |website=South China Morning Post |language=en}}\n\nThe event featured two new title fights for the inaugural [[ONE Lightweight Muay Thai World Championship]] bout between the current [[ONE Lightweight Kickboxing World Championship|ONE Lightweight Kickboxing Champion]] [[Regian Eersel]] vs. [[Sinsamut Klinmee]] and for the inaugural [[ONE Lightweight Submission Grappling World Championship]] bout between the [[ADCC Submission Fighting World Championship#List of ADCC Champions in Men's Submission Fighting by Year and Weight|2022 ADCC World Championship 77kg gold medalist]] [[Kade Ruotolo]] vs. [[Uali Kurzhev]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/regian-eersel-faces-sinsamut-klinmee-at-one-on-prime-video-3/|title=Regian Eersel faces Sinsamut Klinmee at ONE on Prime Video 3|publisher=AsianMMA.com|date=2022-09-08|access-date=2022-10-12}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/submission-grappling/kade-ruotolo-uali-kurzhev-to-fight-for-inaugural-one-lightweight-submission-grappling-world-title/|title=Kade Ruotolo, Uali Kurzhev To Fight For Inaugural ONE Lightweight Submission Grappling World Title|publisher=ONE Championship |author=Ben Coate|date=2022-10-01|access-date=2022-10-12}}\n\nIn the final of the [[2022 in ONE Championship#ONE Muay Thai Flyweight Grand Prix bracket|ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Grand Prix Tournament]] Final bout between [[Superlek Kiatmuu9]] and [[Panpayak Jitmuangnon]] was expected to take place of the event. The winner will be the tournament champion and will be the next challenger [[ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Flyweight Muay Thai title]] against [[Rodtang Jitmuangnon]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.sportskeeda.com/mma/news-superlek-kiatmoo9-panpayak-jitmuangnon-battle-flyweight-muay-thai-world-grand-prix-final|title=Superlek Kiatmoo9 and Panpayak Jitmuangnon battle in the flyweight Muay Thai World Grand Prix final|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Craig Pekios|date=2022-10-11|access-date=2022-10-12}} They met previously 7 times in muay thai at [[Rajadamnern Stadium]] and Phetchbuncha Stadium, with Panpayak winning four fight encounters, Superlek winning two fight and one fight ended from draw.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/th/news/old-foes-superlek-and-panpayak-ready-for-their-8th-face-off-at-the-gp-finals/|title=\"\u0e0b\u0e38\u0e1b\u0e40\u0e1b\u0e2d\u0e23\u0e4c\u0e40\u0e25\u0e47\u0e01 vs \u0e1e\u0e31\u0e19\u0e18\u0e4c\u0e1e\u0e22\u0e31\u0e04\u0e06\u0e4c\" \u0e20\u0e32\u0e04 8 \u0e40\u0e14\u0e34\u0e21\u0e1e\u0e31\u0e19\u0e40\u0e02\u0e47\u0e21\u0e02\u0e31\u0e14 ONE \u0e21\u0e27\u0e22\u0e44\u0e17\u0e22 \u0e40\u0e27\u0e34\u0e25\u0e14\u0e4c \u0e01\u0e23\u0e31\u0e07\u0e14\u0e4c\u0e1b\u0e23\u0e35\u0e0b\u0e4c|publisher=ONE Championship |date=2022-10-04|access-date=2022-10-12}} {{in lang|th}} However, Superlek withdrawn from the event due to training injuries and the bout was postponed.{{Cite web|url=https://www.fightsports.tv/one-on-prime-video-3-update-fighter-injured|title=ONE On Prime Video 3 Update: Fighter Injured|publisher=fightsports.tv|date=2022-10-13|access-date=2022-10-13}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Lea Bivins missed weight, coming in at 116.74 lbs, 1.74 pounds over the limit, Jeremy Miado came in at 127 lbs, 2 pounds over the limit, with both bouts continuing at catchweights and Bivins and Miado paying fines to their opponents. [[Uali Kurzhev]] missed hydration twice and a catchweight of 174 lbs was established, with Kurzhev unable to win the inaugural [[ONE Lightweight Submission Grappling World Championship]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Amazon Prime Video)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[John Lineker]]\n|vs.\n|[[Fabr\u00edcio Andrade]]\n|No Contest (accidental knee to groin)\n|3\n|2:44\n|For the vacant [[ONE Bantamweight World Championship]] (as Lineker missed weight and was stripped the title, only Andrade was eligible). Abbasov vs. Lee\n|image= File:ONE on Prime Video 4.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[ONE Championship]]\n|date={{start date|2022|11|19}}\n|venue=[[Singapore Indoor Stadium]]\n|city=[[Kallang]], Singapore \n|previousevent=[[ONE on Prime Video 3|ONE on Prime Video 3: Lineker vs. Andrade]]\n|followingevent=[[ONE 163|ONE 163: Akimoto vs. Petchtanong]]\n}}\n\n'''''ONE on Prime Video 4: Abbasov vs. Lee''''' (also known as '''''ONE Fight Night 4''''') was a [[Combat sport]] event produced by [[ONE Championship]] that took place on November 19, 2022 at the [[Singapore Indoor Stadium]] in [[Kallang]], Singapore.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/28/23377114/christian-lee-faces-kiamrian-abbasov-in-champ-vs-champ-fight-to-headline-one-on-prime-video-4|title=Christian Lee faces Kiamrian Abbasov in champ vs. champ fight to headline ONE on Prime Video 4|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-09-28|access-date=2022-09-28}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe organization originally targeted a return to [[Ry\u014dgoku Kokugikan]] in [[Tokyo]], Japan to host the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/aung-la-nsang-and-yushin-okami-set-to-fight-in-tokyo/|title=Aung La Nsang and Yushin Okami set to fight in Tokyo|publisher=Asian MMA|author=James Goyder|date=2022-08-11|access-date=2022-08-17}} However, the event was moved to [[Singapore Indoor Stadium]] in [[Kallang]], [[Singapore]] after announced the main event.\n\nA [[ONE Welterweight World Championship]] bout between current champion Kiamrian Abbasov and current [[ONE Lightweight World Championship|ONE Lightweight World Champion]] [[Christian Lee (fighter)|Christian Lee]] (also [[2019 in ONE Championship#ONE Lightweight Grand Prix bracket|2019 ONE Lightweight World Grand Prix Champion]]) headlined the event. At the weigh-ins, Abbasov weighed in at 186.25 pounds, 1.25 pounds over the limit. As a result, Abbasov was stripped of the title and only Lee was eligible to win it.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/11/17/23464412/kiamrian-abbasov-stripped-of-welterweight-title-after-missing-weight-ahead-of-one-on-prime-video-4|title=Kiamrian Abbasov stripped of welterweight title after missing weight ahead of ONE on Prime Video 4|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-11-17|access-date=2022-11-17}}\n\nA [[ONE Flyweight Muay Thai World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Rodtang Jitmuangnon]] and current [[ONE Strawweight Muay Thai World Championship|ONE Strawweight Muay Thai Champion]] [[Joseph Lasiri]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/one-world-champions-joseph-lasiri-rodtang-jitmuangnon-set-to-collide-at-one-on-prime-video-4/|title=ONE World Champions Joseph Lasiri, Rodtang Jitmuangnon Set To Collide At ONE On Prime Video 4|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Bear Farzer|date=2022-09-30|access-date=2022-10-14}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[List of ONE Championship champions#Middleweight World Championship|ONE Middleweight]] and [[List of ONE Championship champions#Light Heavyweight World Championship|Light Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Aung La Nsang]] and former UFC and WSOF Middleweight title contender [[Yushin Okami]] was expected to take place at the event. However, the bout was moved to [[ONE 163]] on same day.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3196334/one-championship-163-aung-la-n-sang-returns-vs-yushin-okami-ham-seo-hee-faces-itsuki-hirata|title=ONE Championship 163: Aung La N Sang returns vs Yushin Okami, Ham Seo-hee faces Itsuki Hirata|publisher=South China Morning Post|author=Tom Taylor|date=2022-10-18|access-date=2022-11-06}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Kwon Won Il]] and Mark Abelardo was scheduled to take the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/kwon-won-il-faces-mark-abelardo-at-one-on-prime-video-4/|title=Kwon Won Il faces Mark Abelardo at ONE on Prime Video 4|publisher=Asian MMA|author=Staff|date=2022-10-25|access-date=2022-11-06}} However, on November 4, it was announced that the bout will be moved to [[ONE 163]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/full-card-revealed-for-one-163-akimoto-vs-petchtanong-on-november-19/|title=Full Card Revealed For ONE 163: Akimoto Vs. Petchtanong On November 19|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Jay Furness|date=2022-11-04|access-date=2022-11-04}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Bibiano Fernandes]], [[Stephen Loman]] and [[Jonathan Haggerty]] all failed hydration test and were forced to take catchweights. Fernandes weighed in at 151.5 lbs, 6.5 pounds over the limit, Loman came in at 153.25 lbs, 8.25 lbs over the bantamweight limit, and Haggerty at 148 lbs, 3 pounds over the limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight with Haggerty being fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent Vladimir Kuzmin. Fernandes and Loman scheduled to meet but to both were unable to make weight in the bantamweight (135 \u2013 145 lbs) and the bout agreed to 153.25 lbs catchweight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3200000/one-champion-prime-video-4-weigh-results-follow-all-updates-singapore|title=ONE Championship on Prime Video 4 weigh-in results: Kiamrian Abbasov loses title on the scale|publisher=South China Morning Post|author=Tom Taylor and Nicolas Atkin|date=2022-11-17|access-date=2022-11-17}}\n\n== Results Anatoly Malykhin at ONE on Prime Video 5|publisher=sportskeeda.com|author=Ben Imperial|date=2022-11-02|access-date=2022-11-02}}\n\nA [[ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Superbon Singha Mawynn]] and former [[List of K-1 champions#K-1 Super Welterweight Championship|K-1 Super Welterweight Champion]] (also [[2022 in ONE Championship#ONE Kickboxing Featherweight Grand Prix bracket|2022 ONE Featherweight Kickboxing World Grand Prix Champion]]) [[Chingiz Allazov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/superbon-vs-allazov-featherweight-kickboxing-world-title-fight-rebooked-for-one-on-prime-video-5/|title=Superbon Vs. Allazov Featherweight Kickboxing World Title Fight Rebooked For ONE On Prime Video 5|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Jay Furness|date=2022-10-28|access-date=2022-10-28}} The pairing was previously scheduled to co-main event at [[ONE on Prime Video 2]], but Allazov forced to withdraw from the event due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/8/16/23307690/superbon-set-to-defend-title-against-chingiz-allazov-at-one-on-prime-video-2-kickboxing-mma|title=Superbon set to defend title against Chingiz Allazov at ONE on Prime Video 2|publisher=MMA Mania|author=Drake Riggs|date=2022-08-16|access-date=2022-10-28}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/chingiz-allazov-out-of-world-title-fight-with-injury-tayfun-ozcan-to-challenge-superbon/|title=Chingiz Allazov Withdraws From World Title Fight; Tayfun Ozcan To Challenge Superbon|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=John Wolcott|date=2022-09-21|access-date=2022-10-28}} In turn, Superbon forced to withdraw due to came down with an illness and the bout was moved to headline at [[ONE on Prime Video 6]] on January 14, 2023.{{Cite web|url=https://combatpress.com/2022/11/superbon-singha-mawynn-vs-chingiz-allazov-moved-to-one-on-prime-video-6/|title=Superbon Singha Mawynn vs. Chingiz Allazov moved to ONE on Prime Video 6|publisher=Combat Press|author=Dan Kuhl|date=2022-11-25|access-date=2022-11-25}}\n\nA [[ONE Women's Atomweight Muay Thai World Championship]] unification bout between current champion [[Allycia Rodrigues]] and interim champion [[Janet Todd (kickboxer)|Janet Todd]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://asianmma.com/allycia-rodrigues-faces-janet-todd-at-one-on-prime-video-5/|title=Allycia Rodrigues faces Janet Todd at ONE on Prime Video 5|publisher=AsianMMA.com|author=Staff|date=2022-10-06|access-date=2022-10-27}} However on November 30, Todd pulled out from the event due to tests positive for [[COVID-19]] and the bout was postponed.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3201551/one-championship-janet-todd-tests-positive-covid-allycia-rodrigues-title-fight-prime-video-5-card|title=ONE Championship: Janet Todd tests positive for Covid, Allycia Rodrigues title fight off Prime Video 5 card|publisher=South China Morning Post|author=Nicolas Atkin|date=2022-11-30|access-date=2022-11-30}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/todd-tests-positive-for-covid-world-title-unification-fight-with-rodrigues-postponed-from-one-on-prime-video-5/|title=Todd Tests Positive For COVID, World Title Unification Fight With Rodrigues Postponed From ONE On Prime Video 5|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Bear Frazer|date=2022-11-30|access-date=2022-12-01}}\n\nA [[ONE Lightweight Submission Grappling World Championship]] bout between current champion [[Kade Ruotolo]] (also the [[ADCC Submission Fighting World Championship#List of ADCC Champions in Men's Submission Fighting by Year and Weight|2022 ADCC World Championship 77kg gold medalist]]) and promotional newcomer Matheus Gabriel that took place the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.onefc.com/news/kade-ruotolo-to-defend-one-lightweight-submission-grappling-world-title-against-matheus-gabriel-on-december-2-2022/|title=Kade Ruotolo To Defend Submission Grappling World Title Against Matheus Gabriel At ONE on Prime Video 5|publisher=ONE Championship \u2013 The Home of Martial Arts|author=Ben Coate|date=2022-11-11|access-date=2022-11-11}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Jackie Buntan]] fails hydration four times and must negotiate a catchweight; she weighed in at 130 pounds, 5 pounds over the strawweight limit. The bout proceeded at catchweight with Buntan being fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent Amber Kitchen.{{Cite web|url=https://www.scmp.com/sport/martial-arts/mixed-martial-arts/article/3201724/one-championship-prime-video-5-weigh-and-hydration-test-results-updates|title=ONE Championship on Prime Video 5 weigh-ins, hydration test results: Jackie Buntan misses mark|publisher=South China Morning Post|author=Tom Taylor and Nicolas Atkin|date=2022-12-01|access-date=2022-12-01}}\n\n== Results
